Proposed small changes, to be confirmed by the journey audit before building: (add) a receipt after a coverage edit saying what changes next and what does not; a Log learning entry on a reading with its outcomes preselected; a first-pass-complete summary matching Home's coverage line with the stated basis of the revision rotation; the mock/final-week context with the exact appointment link; the repair object link from a reading with a weak area. (modify) the exam target picker should reuse the onboarding exam step with a safe return, so target month and booked date stay one rule. (delete) the picker's own exam POST once replaced; any "covered" wording that implies understanding. The first-study, partial-coverage, uncertain prior learning, completed first pass, revision rotation, mock, repair and final-week journeys have not been walked yet.

### 9.19 Calendar integrity slice and Curriculum audit (6 Nov 2026)

**Reproduced on the current checkout, then fixed (one new test: `npm run calendar:integrity:test`, 26 checks, ordinary fixture students, own dev server).**

| Defect | Cause | Fix | Proof |
|---|---|---|---|
| `/app/calendar?week=not-a-date` returned 500 | `weekForDate` ran date math on the raw parameter | `parseLocalDate` (real date, 2020–2100) in `studyWeek.ts`; the page falls back to the current week with a visible notice | `not-a-date`, `2026-02-30`, `9999-12-31` all 200 with the notice |

| A mock could be booked over a class, commitment or timed task | `saveCheckpoint`/`addAppointment` never checked the clock time | `assertSlotFree` before the command: 409 `SLOT_TAKEN`, nothing changed, other times offered; a mock without a time still holds the day only | mock over a task refused with suggestions; adjacent mock accepted |

### 9.20 Timed bookings at the write boundary, preparation review, Curriculum evidence (6 Nov 2026)

Evidence kinds are marked: **[isolated SQL]** two real connections on a throwaway full schema; **[live HTTP]** ordinary fixture students on the dev project; **[browser]** Playwright, one fixture student; **[unit]**; **[code]** read only.

**1. Timed bookings (migration 20261128, applied to dev).** BEFORE/AFTER triggers on `mock_appointments`, `plan_blocks`, `calendar_commitments`, `commitment_exceptions` run after the existing `scope_write_lock`, so every writer (checkpoint command, task create/move/resize, preparation, redistribution, class and exception edits) is covered at the shared boundary; the TypeScript check stays as a preflight that supplies suggestions. A timed mock is a hard conflict with a protected occurrence (one-off, weekly with bounds, skipped and moved occurrences, midnight-spanning), another timed mock or a timed task; a timed task conflicts with a timed mock only (task-to-task overlap remains a warning); a class/commitment edit or moved occurrence cannot land on a booked mock. Half-open intervals; instants compared in the student's timezone; unchanged bookings and label-only edits are not re-checked. Refusal is SQLSTATE `BK409`, mapped to the same 409 (`SLOT_TAKEN` / `CONFLICT`) in `checkpointService.apply`, `moveTask` and `POST /api/tasks`; the transaction (receipt, outcome) rolls back; replay returns the existing receipt first. **[isolated SQL]** `npm run booking:race:test` 50/50: mock vs mock, mock then task, task then mock, mock then protected occurrence, protected occurrence then mock (second writer waits on the lock, is refused, exactly one booking), adjacent contenders both succeed, other students never wait, 12 mixed concurrent writers (no deadlock, no unexpected error), refused/postponed command leaves appointment, outcomes and receipts unchanged, same command succeeds when the slot is freed. Calendar dialog now lists free times from a refused booking. **[browser, 390 px]** booking over the fixture's Work block shows "Work is already there…"; 18:00 saves.

**2. Preparation review (migration 20261129, applied).** Defect reproduced (`[[false,null]]`): review was inferred from the projected suggestion date, which equals the task date after a one-day move. Now `checkpoint_suggestion_tasks.basis_date` records the appointment date the student accepted or kept (set by trigger on add and on "Keep it"); `loadRelated` asks when that appointment's own date differs, or it is gone, for any planned appointment, not only the next. Task rows are never touched; the Open link uses the dated task route. **[live HTTP]** `e2e:checkpoint:commands` 123/0 (2 skipped, one proved by `checkpoint:rehearse`): setup writes asserted; rename does not ask; move asks naming both dates; Keep acknowledges; unrelated edit after Keep does not ask; a later move asks again; a second, non-next appointment's preparation follows its own schedule; cancellation reason; completed preparation never offered. Existing rows were backfilled with their current appointment date (history cannot say what was accepted earlier).

**3. Curriculum.** **C1 (migration 20261130, applied).** A trigger on `user_module_state` refuses a `self_report` write that replaces a verified or diagnostic-owned row, or creates self-reported "verified"; class, task-completion and diagnostic writers are unaffected; `evidence_events` is never touched by the route. The route pre-reads (503 `UNAVAILABLE` on a failed read, distinct from 400 invalid input), rejects duplicates, caps 200, returns 409 `VERIFIED_COVERAGE_PROTECTED` with a link to the reading, and one upsert statement keeps a batch atomic. **[live HTTP]** `curriculum:coverage:test` 21/21: seeded verified row unchanged byte-for-byte, mixed batch saves nothing, fabrication refused by route and database, ordinary and batch self-reports save, diagnostic can still decay/verify, concept/evidence/repair/attempt counts unchanged, Readiness repair links. Older overwritten rows identifiable from trustworthy history (a self_report row with verification passes or a verified timestamp): **0** on the dev project; no reconstruction proposed. **Exam change**: `POST /api/roadmap` no longer writes the exam (410 `USE_EXAM_EDITOR`; the only caller was the retired `ExamTargetPicker`, deleted); Curriculum links to `/onboarding/exam?returnTo=/app/curriculum` (Curriculum added to the return allowlist). **Actions**: Roadmap coverage edit and rebuild show pending, saved ("your own report, not test evidence; this week's accepted tasks are unchanged; the roadmap and next weekly review use it") or a recoverable error. **Readiness** rows open the dated Calendar task when a repair is scheduled, the Coach repair view when open and unplaced, else the reading. **[browser]** reading page → Log learning opens with Economics → reading 12 preselected; Roadmap "Change exam" opens the shared editor with the return; a coverage edit showed the receipt and persisted. Not browser-checked: verified-row protection message (the UI locks verified rows; protection proven over HTTP/SQL), Readiness links.

Joined experience.** `npm run verify` exit 0 (includes route audit; run before the late Curriculum/UI edits; TypeScript clean after them). **[live HTTP, actual bank]** e2e:entry 46/0, revision 59/0, weekly-review 31/0, home:journeys 45/0, home:assessments 24/0, coach 44/0, class-log 60/0, learning-log 13/13, onboarding 90/0, checkpoint 75/0, calendar-integrity 26/0 (re-run with the guard), e2e:calendar 10/4 (all "bank has 0 unseen items"). ### 9.21 Booking gaps, historical Calendar, fixture-bank runs, baseline (6 Nov 2026)

**Booking gaps (migration 20261201, applied; 20261128–20261130 untouched).** Restoring an occurrence is now a booking: an AFTER DELETE trigger on `commitment_exceptions` re-checks the reinstated class against timed mocks under the same lock (no app code deletes exceptions today; the path is DB/support/cascade). Commitment-side refusals now put the booked **mock** in DETAIL and the occurrence in HINT; `POST /api/commitments` and `PATCH /api/commitments/[id]` (series edit and moved occurrence) return 409 `BOOKING_CONFLICT` naming both ("Dentist would overlap Mock Booked…"), with nothing saved; the editors keep the student's input and show the message (existing error paths). Redistribution maps `BK409` to "The longer session would run into your booked mock, so nothing moved." Infrastructure errors still return 500. Same-day mock edits: preparation stays date-based; a same-day change that runs into a timed prep task is refused by the booking guard (409 naming it), other same-day changes and label-only edits save without asking. **[isolated SQL]** `booking:race:test` 59/59 (adds: restore skipped/moved over a booked mock refused with both names and records intact; archive and hard-delete of the series still work; restore-vs-book races in both orders). **[live HTTP]** `calendar:integrity:test` 42/42 (adds commitment 409s, series/occurrence edits unchanged, same-day edits, history/future/current period checks).

**Historical Calendar.** The viewed period decides the page: a past week shows its work, a "past week" notice with completion counts, its mocks/checkpoints (status, designation, debrief link) and an "Assessment that week" summary with the result link; no add-mock prompt, paper strip or current actions. A week beyond the current cycle is labelled a forecast with no current scope. An unknown/foreign `appointment=` link says it is unavailable instead of showing the next mock. Unplaced work shows only in the display week holding its cycle's start (or the week holding today for the cycle in progress). **[live HTTP]** covered in the 42 checks. Not browser-checked at desktop/390 px after this change (earlier closed-week screenshot predates it).

**Fixture-bank track.** A fixture bank (204 items, 6 readings, rehearsal families) was created and destroyed by its record. Only `e2e-rehearsal-journey` honours `--bank`; its first booking was over the fixture's Work block, so its start time moved to 19:00 (the new rule is correct): **83 passed / 2 failed** (Home appointment markup assertion; "three concurrent starts" returned `insufficient` for two of three). The other scored suites (`e2e-calendar`, `-paper-journey`, `-taking-flow`, `-results`, `-readiness`) ignore the flag and ran the **actual-bank** track: unchanged failures, all "bank has 0 unseen items" or stale assertions. The scored paper → result → repair → closure click-through, in-app rehearsal closure, lost-accept replay and stale-scope second tab were **not** run in the browser.

**Baseline.** `beta:baseline` regenerated (88 migrations covered); `beta:install:rehearse` identical to source in every category, `beta:restore:rehearse` 10/10, `beta:admission:test` 15/15. Upgrade from the previous baseline: the isolated booking suite installs the old baseline and applies 20261128–20261201 on top through the checksum runner. `npm run verify` exit 0 and `tsc` clean on the final tree.

### 9.22 Scored journeys, rehearsal start race, answer-save recovery, Coach agreement (6 Nov 2026)

Evidence kinds: **[HTTP]** live HTTP on ordinary fixture students; **[browser]** Playwright; **[SQL]** isolated schema; **[unit]**. Categories kept apart: *product defect* (fixed), *stale assertion*, *missing inventory*.

**Bank modes (`scripts/lib/bankMode.ts`).** The scored suites `e2e-calendar`, `-taking-flow`, `-results`, `-readiness` now call `declareBank`: no flag = ACTUAL track; `--bank=<record>` = FIXTURE track, validated up front (file parses, items exist) or rejected. It prints track, target and routine-eligible counts per reading. Actual track: too little routine inventory exits 0 as **UNAVAILABLE** (never counted as passed). Fixture track: missing coverage exits 1. Routine and held-out fixtures are separate records (`fixture-bank create --readings=…` vs `--rehearsal=auto`; the rehearsal mode produces held-out families only). The record's creator owns cleanup; all three records were destroyed (0 items/concepts/users left).
**Results, fixture track [HTTP]:** e2e-readiness **25/0** (was 14/11 on the actual track), e2e-results **57/0**, e2e-taking-flow **65/0** (two stale single-paper-mock assertions updated to the retired-mock contract), e2e-calendar **26/0, 1 skipped** ("every topic has reviewed questions": needs all-topic routine coverage), e2e-rehearsal-journey **86/0** (held-out record). Not asserted: that routine papers draw the fixture items specifically (only the rehearsal test checks this).

**Product defect fixed: rehearsal start race.** Reproduced earlier (two of three concurrent starts returned `insufficient`). Cause as suspected: the losing request read "no open rehearsal", a competitor pinned both forms, the loser then loaded a smaller held-out pool. `startRehearsal` now resolves an open reservation for the same owned appointment before returning a shortfall (and after `BANK_CHANGED`). **[HTTP, forced interleaving via the `betweenReads` seam]** `rehearsal:start:race` fixture track **10/10**: the loser resumes the same rehearsal; one rehearsal, two forms, two attempts, no deadline until a session starts; forms use fixture items; 5 concurrent starts ×2 rounds return one rehearsal; a foreign appointment creates nothing; a cancelled one is `closed`; a second appointment never resumes the first's reservation. Actual-bank mode of the same script (genuine shortfall starts nothing, offers Calendar) is implemented; not run on its own this session.
**Home link.** Home shows the next appointment through ProgressSummary; the assertion now checks that link carries the appointment id and opens that appointment in Calendar, with the other appointment in the same schedule (no redundant Home list restored). A stale "practice must not start" assertion now checks that no held-out item is drawn (0 of 10 drawn on a routine reading).

**Product defect fixed: answer saving was not recoverable.** `AttemptRunner.save` had no catch, left "Saving…" on a transport failure, and showed no retry. Now: a per-question sequence ignores delayed responses for older choices; a failed or lost response shows "Not saved" with the student's selection and confidence kept and a **Retry saving** button (also on the last question); retry is safe (idempotent per question). The route logs a failed after-commit progress write by stage and returns the answer as saved with `progressSaved:false`. **[browser]** with the answer request aborted: "Not saved", alert, selection kept, Retry shown; fault lifted, Retry → "Saved", Next unlocked; and with the server committing but the response dropped: same recovery; the database held one row per question (2 rows, 2 distinct items). **[HTTP]** `answer:save:contention` 19/19 over 3 rounds (42 saves, p50 280 ms, p95 447 ms, 0 failures): concurrent saves of different questions, a client-aborted request (committed once or absent, retry leaves exactly one row), rapid concurrent changes to one question (one row), submit with no loss or duplicates. `attempt:race` 8/8. **The earlier two statement timeouts are still unclassified:** none reproduced; this load shows durable-before-response. Not injected: a database failure before the write (would be a 500 with request id, nothing saved) and a failing progress write (handled by the new branch, not forced).

**Product defect fixed: Coach wording could state an invented time.** `coach:provider:agreement` (stub provider on 127.0.0.1, `NVIDIA_BASE_URL` now honoured as documented): an answer saying "Saturday at 03:00" was accepted. New validator rule `invented_schedule` rejects clock times and calendar dates in model wording (times come from Calendar links); eval case added (`coach:eval` 29/29, `coach:actions:test` 43/43). **[HTTP]** 7/7: with a valid answer, malformed answer, slow provider (cut at the 12 s model timeout) and unreachable provider, links, proposals and intent are identical; no schedule row changed and no proposal accepted by asking; fallback reasons reported separately (`invented_schedule`, `not_json`, `timeout`, `provider_error`). **Live provider (configured key, 2 synthetic questions):** both returned `empty` after ~12 s, so deterministic fallback; no validated live wording was obtained (cause unclassified: endpoint, quota or model).

**Measurement.** `tests:page:measure` now logs inventory: with fixtures present total 840, routine eligible 352, held-out eligible 204, fixture items 537, student-seen 0. Routine-fixtures-present run: sequential n=10 p50 1.9 s p95 3.9 s; contention 8×2 (n=16) p50 4.0 s p95 7.3 s; all 200. Earlier actual-bank run (303 total, no fixtures): p50 2.6 s p95 3.3 s / p50 4.2 s p95 5.5 s. Read counts and a realistic-history run were **not** captured; the 89 s request is still unexplained. The earlier "303" was total inventory, not eligibility.

**Stale assertions fixed (verified against current behaviour):** e2e:roadmap 42/0, e2e:editing 40/0 (booking day now the weekend; Work blocks weekdays). `e2e:tests-scope` with guarded flags: 19 + 13 scenarios passed. `npm run verify` exit 0, TypeScript clean, 0 pending migrations (no schema change this slice; baseline not regenerated).

**Not done.** Browser journeys at desktop and 390 px for: fresh self-study with a scored paper, Keep for later → Review follow-up without reload, mock in a month, class moved after preview, final week/exam follow-up, external-mock and rehearsal closure, lost accept response, stale paper scope in a second tab (their HTTP-level suites pass; browser proof is missing). ### 9.23 Notes safety, answer ordering, bank validation, Curriculum correctness (7 Nov 2026)

Evidence kinds: **[browser]** Playwright (one fixture student per journey, deleted by recorded id); **[HTTP]** ordinary fixture students; **[SQL]** isolated full schema; **[unit]**. Categories: *defect fixed*, *stale assertion*, *not done*.

**Migrations (all additive, applied to dev, baseline regenerated to 91 covered, install/restore/admission rehearsals pass):** 20261202 `answer_ordering` (client_seq + stamped `save_attempt_response`), 20261203 `atomic_roadmap_replacement` (`replace_roadmap`, service-only), 20261204 Ethics summary wording. 20261128–20261201 untouched. 20261202/1203 were first applied on the isolated full schema (`booking:race:test` 59/59 installs them on the previous baseline).

**Notes (N1–N4, N5 partly, C8).** *Defect fixed N1 (P0):* the editor now saves serially and acknowledges the exact document sent; a failure never overwrites a newer draft with an older one. **[browser]** older text saving, newer typed while pending, first save dropped: server holds both ("OLDTEXT NEWTEXT"), status "Saved". *N2:* a conflict keeps both versions with three choices (copy my text / use the saved version / keep mine and replace it); a retry after a committed save is recognised as saved (`replayed`, key-order-independent comparison). **[browser]** another tab's save was not overwritten; local text stayed on screen. *N3:* a 400 is not retried (1 request) and shows the reason plus **Retry**; transient failures retry at most 3 times then wait for Retry. *N4:* the validator rejects non-array marks, non-object attrs/marks, text on non-text nodes with a structured 400 (`notes:safety:test` 14/14). *N5:* every unsaved state is kept per signed-in user and note in localStorage and offered back ("Restore it / Discard it") after a refresh; `beforeunload` now covers unsaved, saving, error and conflict **[browser: warning shown, banner after reload]**. Not done: internal-navigation interception beyond unmount flush, IME-specific tests, account-switch draft test (key includes the user id; not exercised). *C8 (partly):* the notes GET/POST distinguish an unavailable read (503) from a missing note; reading and subject pages show a section failure instead of a blank editor when the note read fails; flashcard review reports a failed load.

**Answer ordering (reliability §2).** *Defect reproduced and fixed:* a choice arriving after a newer committed choice replaced it (answer, confidence, last question and finalisation all wrong: 7 of 11 checks failed). The runner stamps each save (`clientSeq`, monotonic, wall-clock ordered across tabs); the RPC refuses an older stamp (SQLSTATE ST409 → 409 `stale_answer` carrying the stored answer, which the runner shows with a message); the same stamp is an idempotent retry; unstamped callers keep last-write. **[HTTP]** `answer:ordering:test` 11/11 (answer and confidence edits, lost-response retry, replay after a newer choice, last question, finalisation, no duplicate rows, closed attempt refuses); contention 19/19 (0 failures in 42 saves); `attempt:race` 8/8; e2e-results 57/57, e2e-taking-flow 65/65 (routine fixture); rehearsal journey 86/86. Two tabs: the later-stamped choice wins; the earlier tab is told and shows the saved answer.

**Bank validation (reliability §1).** `bankMode.ts` now reads the WHOLE record in batches of 100 (a read error is a setup failure), requires every id present, fixture-only and `reviewed`, derives counts from the record, enforces the pool (routine vs held-out), and excludes fixture items from actual-bank counts. **[HTTP, negative paths]** a routine record lacking r027/r033 fails the readiness suite although the database holds items for them; a record with a missing id fails as stale. `rehearsal:start:race` uses it (held-out pool) and its **actual-bank mode ran independently: 3/3** (a real shortfall starts nothing, offers Calendar); the fixture race still passes 10/10. Not done: a held-out-record-to-routine-suite negative run; asserting that routine papers draw the intended fixture items.

**Curriculum (C1, C2, C3, C7, C9, C10 partly).** *C2 fixed:* repair threads in `in_progress` and `awaiting_recheck` no longer drop out of Readiness; a thread without observations stays **Repair with sample 0** (it no longer fabricates sample 1). **[HTTP]** all five live states plus closed (`curriculum:coverage:test`, section 8). *C3:* the fixed pass marks and question counts (≥90%, 12–15, next-day retest) are replaced by the real closing rule (a fresh, eligible check that comes out supported, scheduled by Roadmap) and no fixed numbers; not done: the sure-and-wrong hypothesis wording and due-time display. *C1:* the ambiguous "Covered %" ring is replaced by "X of Y readings reported covered / Z partly covered, your own report, not test evidence". *C7 fixed:* flashcard review carries `expectedReps` and compare-and-sets, so a retry or concurrent identical rating does not advance a card twice; the runner shows failures and keeps the card (`curriculum:coverage:test` section 9; ratings leave evidence counts unchanged). *C9 fixed:* `generateRoadmap` calls `replace_roadmap` (one transaction under `scope_lock`: supersede, insert, all milestones or nothing; same input hash returns the existing roadmap). **[HTTP]** a failing milestone rolls everything back with the previous roadmap and milestones intact; `e2e:roadmap` 42/42. *C10:* the Ethics "heaviest single topic" claim corrected (data migration + catalogue). **Not done:** the Portfolio Management label (the official 2027 name could not be verified offline), C4–C6 and C11–C12 UX (progressive disclosure, subject search, notebook structure, exact-link audit), the My notes route, entry migration, search, archive/export, briefs and mistake notes (slices C1–C4 of the master prompt).

**Coach live provider.** Probe (`coach:provider:probe`, one synthetic request, metadata only): with the production budget (500 tokens, JSON format) the provider returned HTTP 200, `finish_reason: length`, all 500 completion tokens spent on reasoning, **no visible content**, which is the earlier "empty" fallback. One variable changed: budget 1500 → `stop`, 120 visible chars; 3000 → `stop`. Coach now uses 2000. Through the route (live key): one question returned **validated live wording** (fallback false, 316 chars), one fell back on the 12 s timeout (provider latency, not content). The stub agreement run is separate evidence of fallback behaviour.

**Measurement.** `tests:page:measure` now separates raw reviewed counts from eligibility computed by the serving path (`loadEligibleItemPool`, `countFreshByReading`). Fixture r028/r029 run, no-history student, dev server: raw reviewed routine 109, held-out 204; eligible for this student over r028+r029: routine 90, held-out 10; fresh per reading 45/45. Sequential n=10 p50 2.3 s p95 3.0 s; contention 8×2 (n=16) p50 4.9 s p95 6.2 s; all 200. Read counts per request and a representative-history student were not captured; the 89 s request and the earlier answer timeouts remain unexplained.

**Browser journeys (reliability §3).** Clicked: Curriculum reading with notes (above); a practice attempt at **390 px**: answer alone keeps Next locked ("Now choose how sure you are"), answer + confidence saves, refresh returns to the same attempt with Q1 retained, all questions answered, submit reaches the practice result ("not recorded as evidence", Open Calendar), no horizontal scroll. ### 9.25 Tests revamp — integrity closeout and student experience (9 Nov 2026)

Brief: the "Next Tests slice" prompt after §9.24 (T4 plus the minimum T1). Evidence files: screenshots and logs in the session scratchpad.

**Integrity (Pass A).** Migrations `20261207_challenge_score_ledger`, `20261208_challenge_seen_items`, `20261209_challenge_seen_items_fix`.
- `resolve_item_challenge` used to change evidence but never the stored score; it now calls `_rescore_attempt` (internal, no student execute), the single SQL ledger for an adjustment. It keeps each form's own policy (`1.0` stays answered-only, `paper-score.1` stays over delivered items), stores `scoreable_count`, bumps `result_revision`, and writes `attempt_score_adjustments` (old/new values, idempotent per challenge). Required example verified live: 2 answered / 1 correct / 6 omitted = 13% becomes 7 scoreable / 1 answered / 6 omitted / 1 voided = 14% (1/7), delivered stays 8.
- Validity lives in `item_challenges` (pending = still scoreable and provisional; resolved_voided = set aside once; resolved_kept = valid), not in `evidence_events`, so practice papers and omitted items work. Lock order is attempt row then challenge row in `finalize`, `resolve` and `raise`; finalize skips already-voided items and marks pending ones provisional before any repair decision. Proven with three submit-vs-resolve races in both orders.
- A student can report a question they have OPENED before answering (a progress row or a started attempt is the proof); an unopened reserved question, a foreign attempt and an item not on the form are refused with one message. `20261208` was wrong (`record is not null`); `20261209` corrects it.
- `finalize` and `resolve` were patched from their LIVE definitions. Install-vs-dev reconciliation compares md5 of every function body: identical (96 functions). Baseline regenerated (96 migrations); install and restore rehearsals pass.
- Combined mocks: one contract (`combineLedgers`); sessions under different or unknown policies show NO combined score, each session alone; an expired session's policy and size come from its reserved form.
- As-of evidence: the result's findings and SWOT come from that paper's own observations (installed `outcomeVerdict`, thresholds unchanged, same tie-break as Curriculum); cumulative status is a separately labelled "Current status" with a timestamp; concept evidence is bounded to when the paper was scored, paged, chunked, and a failed read is unavailable, not empty. One or two successes are labelled "Observed success, more evidence needed" (wording only).

**Experience (Passes B–D).** `/app/tests` has `view=week|practice|mocks` (validated, default week), one primary stateful card (Review and start opens the read-only preflight; only Start reserves), scope detail behind a disclosure (open when confirmation is needed), paginated history with a stable (date, id) cursor, and read-failure/exam-sat states. Practice is one selection flow (reading check, subject check, baseline with explicit opt-in, timed practice) with preview first; an outcome-specific Curriculum link opens timed practice because only practice honours one outcome. Mocks view shows a short capability line with details collapsed. The runner skips blank questions, protects an unsaved choice (visible reason, retry, or explicit discard that never clears a server answer), shows review counts, offers a safe exit, hides the Coach launcher and clears the menu button. Durable rehearsal settlement moved out of the hub page and the GET api into `POST /api/rehearsal/[id]/session { action: 'settle' }`; expiry also bypasses the confidence gate on submit. Result: hero (score basis, completion, one finding, one next action) plus four disclosures; omitted and flagged questions are reviewable; challenge status shows pending/upheld/dismissed. Links: Curriculum `?los=` (validated), Coach `?attempt=` (owned, scored; page, card, API and context loader), Result to the reading and its note section.

**Proof.** `challenge:ledger:db:test` 26/26; `score:ledger:test` 11; `reviewer:db:test` 60; `paper:db:test` 37; `paper:score:db:test` 8; `attempt:race` 8; `repair:db:test` 35; `answer:ordering:test` 11; `e2e-results` 63; `e2e-taking-flow` 65; `e2e-paper-journey` 49; `e2e-rehearsal-journey` 92; `e2e-reviewer` 53; `e2e-coach` 44; `npm run verify` exit 0. ### 9.27 T3 difficulty adjustment and bounded adaptive practice (11 Nov 2026)

Everything below is proven on **synthetic fixture banks**; synthetic labels exercise software branching, not CFA calibration. Real bank: 19 items marked reviewed (legacy, no recorded approval), **0 reviewer-confirmed difficulty levels**, 0 approved, 0 source packs. Nothing was approved, no rights cleared, no concept signed off.

- **Pass A (migration 20261215).** `item_difficulty_reviews` (per exact item version: level, rationale ≥10 chars, reviewer, time; history kept, latest wins), view `item_confirmed_difficulty`, command `confirm_item_difficulty` (reviewer only, stale-version refused). `decide_item_review` was patched in place so a NEW approval requires a confirmed level for that version; earlier approvals are not backfilled and stay usable as "unclassified" (never counted as Standard, never satisfying a reviewed quota). Reviewer UI: level + cognitive-demand rationale + history on the item workspace; route `/api/diagnostic/items/[id]/difficulty`. Empirical statistics: unavailable, no fields.
- **Freezing.** A BEFORE INSERT trigger on `form_items` records `difficulty_level/basis/review_id` in the reservation's own transaction. Reclassifying later never relabels an earlier paper.
- **Pass B.** `src/lib/tests/difficultyPolicy.ts` (`difficulty-policy.1`): profiles support/balanced/challenge over confirmed foundation/standard/difficult (stretch excluded); evidence = valid, scored, evidence-mode, confirmed-family, first-exposure, frozen-confirmed-level observations, one per family, newest 20 inside `STALE_DAYS` (21); ≥8 observations over ≥2 papers; per reading (scope evidence first, then labelled broader-reading evidence, else cold-start Balanced); one step at a time; changes held until 2 papers after a change. Timing and omissions are never inputs. Mocks, re-checks and fixed timed practice keep their fixed policy (no difficulty summary).
- **Pass C.** `assembleEligiblePool` is now a coverage-first bipartite matching (family trap solved: 200 seeds, no false shortage), soft integer difficulty target, unclassified reported separately, seeded tie-break (preview = reservation). `start_paper_checked` re-checks, under the student's lock, the evidence revision (`difficulty_evidence_version`) and every item's confirmed level; a mismatch returns `DECISION_CHANGED` → stale preview. The form records policy, targets, actual mix, evidence revision and seed. Results show the delivered mix and whether the approach changed. Staleness is conservative: any scored-evidence change counts as "material" (shortcut).
- **Pass D.** `practice_runs`/`practice_run_sets` (students: select own only), `practice_run_reserve`/`practice_run_stop` (service role, one advisory lock per student), `/api/tests/preflight|start` with `mode=adaptive`, `/api/tests/practice-runs/[id]` (+`/next`, `/stop`). Sets of five, cap 10/20, untimed ordinary practice attempts, practice-only profile replay (two full sets ≥80% with ≥2 independent standard successes and few mismatches → up one; two answered sets ≤50% → down one; omissions never lower). UI: Adaptive practice in Practice and checks (`?mode=adaptive` link allowlisted), preview, set result with next-set reasoning, Continue/Stop/summary.
- **Proof.** `difficulty:policy:test` 45; `e2e-difficulty-journey` 61/61 (endpoints, races, purity, evidence exclusion); reviewer-db 63, paper-db 37, batch-integrity 35, worker-sim 12, progress-db 10, challenge-ledger 28, score-ledger 15, result 34, tests:scope 58, paper/rehearsal contracts 24/17. Install and restore rehearsals pass (baseline covers 102 migrations). Browser (Playwright, desktop then 390 px, no horizontal overflow): Curriculum-style link → preview → Start → five answers with required confidence → set result with next-set reason → reload → Continue → open-set resume → partly answered set → capped summary; lost Start response retried to one run.
- **Not done / open.** Browser proof of: second-tab Continue/Stop, scope shortage, weekly difficulty card, reviewer level UI, and the three T2 chains (insufficient-bank Week, stale-scope second tab, result → repair → Calendar). Concept-level evidence tier not built (outcome then reading). `audit:routes` currently exits 1 on a dead `/auth/error` link in the owner's uncommitted auth edits (not touched here). `/progress` 29–70 s heartbeat cause and the concurrent-suite failure remain unclassified. - **C1.** `audit:routes` failed on `/api/tests/practice-runs/x/x` (templated `${kind}`), not on auth. `AdaptiveRunPanel` now makes two literal calls (`/next`, `/stop`); a mutated `/halt` is flagged, so the audit still catches a genuinely missing endpoint. `npm run verify` exits 0 (47 steps).
- **C2/C3.** `decideReadingProfile`: scope sample is selected, then capped at 20; the broader-reading sample is a separate capped sample. Hysteresis counts papers completed AFTER the change paper (implicit Balanced start; chronological with id tie-break). Recorded as `POLICY_PATCH = scope-cap-hold.1` beside `difficulty-policy.1` on summaries; earlier forms keep their frozen interpretation. Mixed readings read "Adjusted by reading; open details for the differences."
- **C4.** Migration `20261216_practice_receipts_and_level_lock`: `practice_run_receipts` (user, key, op, run, block, attempt, scope hash; backfilled), `practice_run_receipt()` resolved before inventory/cap/stopped checks. Same key + different scope/run/op → `key_conflict`; another student's key is not a receipt. `replayable()` re-checks the receipt after any failure (concurrent same-key).
- **C5/C6.** `chain()` is scope-relevant (only questions on the planned outcomes count; sets with none are dropped; an open set elsewhere no longer truncates later sets), bulk-read (no per-set N+1) and throws on failed reads. `practiceSetStats` excludes voided/disputed questions, repeated/familiar families (earlier sets or earlier valid evidence) and cannot let a reduced sample count as a full set.
- **Pass B.** `start_paper_checked` and `practice_run_reserve` take FOR KEY SHARE on the selected question rows before comparing confirmed levels (`_lock_check_levels`); `confirm_item_difficulty` already takes FOR UPDATE, so a reclassification can no longer commit between the comparison and the freeze (control run reproduces the old window). Evidence/scope writers already serialise on `scope_lock(user)` via `scope_write_lock` triggers. `scripts/difficulty-race-db-test.cjs`: 28 checks.
- **Browser (Playwright, desktop + 390 px, no overflow):** reviewer level UI (found and fixed a hydration mismatch in level history), weekly "Why this mix" by keyboard, second-tab Continue/Stop (stale Stop did not end the run or the open set), stale-scope second tab ("Your completed work changed since you reviewed it", 0 attempts), result → repair preview → accept → Calendar link, insufficient-bank Week on the actual empty bank.

### 9.29 Coach revamp — first slice (12 Nov 2026)

Delivered (software, staging-proven; nothing committed):
- `src/lib/coach/interpret.ts` (`coach-interpret-2026-11-12.1`): normalised copy of the message (original kept), typo/contraction fixes that never touch digits or ids, negation and hypotheticals for report-type intents only, corrections, multi-intent, a teaching-component detector that separates "teach me derivatives" from "teach me how to organise my revision", a stated day word that is text only, and a few high-precision Hinglish phrases.
- `src/lib/coach/capabilities.ts`: one registry (execute / handoff / boundary / model) read by the API, quick actions and tests. Teaching is not advertised.
- `/api/coach`: the client `intent` is an untrusted hint that can only choose between intents the message really contains; a mixed request answers the planning part and declines the teaching part; optional `taskId` (owned, live) gives one reviewed single-task move through the existing `confirm_coach_proposal`; unreadable sources are named in the reply instead of reported as empty.
- Migration `20261217_coach_turns`: `turn_key` on `coach_messages`, `coach_turn_begin` (replay / resume / atomic hourly allowance counted once) and `coach_turn_finish` (answer + proposals written together, once; a late answer after Clear adds nothing).
- UI: three suggestions, scope line, failed input kept with Retry on the same turn key, IME-safe Enter, Clear failure shown, history failure shown as unavailable, `?task=` selection card.
- Effect wording: a deferral does not place work on another day (today's and future deferrals are "set aside"); the receipt reports deferred versus minutes placed, from `redistribution_receipts`.

Not done in this slice (stated, not hidden): bounded model interpreter and versioned prompt/schema package; structured response blocks; rehearsal/appointment/proposal-as-structured-context selection; tentative-vs-booked exam, availability and class-change previews (handoffs only); Journeys A–C as durable scripts; the class-log/mock browser matrix; reviewer stale-level, Keep-for-later, adaptive-shortage browser proof; Coach stage timing and read counts; the three source-pack drafts and review index.

### 9.30 Coach slice 2 — language, context and reliable actions (13 Nov 2026)

Delivered (software, staging-proven; nothing committed). Prompt/schema package and change log: `docs/COACH-PROMPTS.md`.
- **Interpreter fixes** (each failing test written first): a day belongs to the requested action ("Move it to Friday because I am busy today" → Friday; reason clauses are not targets); identifier-like tokens (`task-tdy`, hyphen/underscore) are never autocorrected; calculation/solution requests without the word "teach" ("How do I compute bond duration?") are out of scope; `next <weekday>` and "kal" ask instead of guessing.
- **Bounded model interpreter** (`interpretModel.ts`, `prompts.ts`): strict schema, spans must occur in the original message, no URL/SQL/id-shaped text, no confidence, model teaching flags count only where the grammar agrees, a "reason" date is never the target, negation can only remove an action. Runs only for unresolved wording. The server re-resolves everything.
- **Genuine rescheduling**: a selected not-started task + a day → one exact preview (`move_task`, real date and a free 15-minute-grid slot, or an explicitly labelled day-only placement) → confirm → `confirm_coach_move` (one transaction: Calendar's revision / past-day / cycle / continued / recorded-learning / protected-booking rules, slot freedom, event, receipt) → link to the task on its new day. It is not a deferral; deferral keeps its own command and wording. A correction ("No, not Sunday, Saturday") drops the old preview. "yes" confirms exactly one fresh visible proposal through the same command, otherwise asks. Pending clarification (choices, day) is stored with the answer and survives reload.
- **Turn integrity** (migration `20261218_coach_turn_integrity_and_move`): turn key bound to a hash of message + task + attempt + reading (reuse with another payload → 409, nothing written); one owner per in-flight turn by lease token, late workers fenced at finish and at every model reservation; model requests reserved before dispatch in `coach_model_calls`, counted separately from turns, not reset by Clear; Clear is one transaction under the per-student lock; dismiss is one conditional write and never reports a confirmed proposal as dismissed; replay fetches the exact owned answer + proposals by id and returns the stored interpreter/prompt versions and fallback status.
- **Gateway**: reserve → dispatch → abort at deadline (the deadline wins even if a provider ignores the signal) → settle; ≤ 2 requests and 20 s per turn; indeterminate outcomes recorded, not guessed.
- **Selection**: `reading` added to the validated selection contract (card, API, hash, link first, "notes are not evidence"); an unknown reading is "not available", never a fallback. Choice links now prefill the question (the client remounts when the selection changes; before, a same-route navigation kept the old state and the prefill was silently lost).
- **Fixes found while proving it**: choice-link overflow at 390 px (6 px) fixed; the model copied an earlier refusal onto a study-method question (history now omits refusals; response prompt states method help is in scope; version bump); a model-flagged "teaching" on method help no longer refuses it; `next` with no available paper no longer sends the student to "this week's paper".
- **Provider**: reasoning-on made every live call exceed its deadline; reasoning-off (measured) answers in about 1–2 s.

Proof (staging project asylsxszdhlnzewhwtga; ordinary throwaway students removed by recorded id; zero eligible approved bank, fixtures not needed for these checks):
- `coach:interpret:test` 90, `coach:actions:test` 43, `coach:eval` 44, `coach:interp:eval` hold-out (see COACH-PROMPTS.md), `coach:selection:test` 13, `coach:turns:db:test` 14, `coach:integrity:db:test` 40 (payload binding, lease expiry + fencing, budget survives Clear, atomic Clear, move rules, forced failure rolls back, 6 concurrent confirms → one event, edit/confirm and confirm/clear races: both outcomes seen in confirm/clear, only "edit first" in edit/confirm), `coach:turns:runtime:test` 25 (SIMULATED counted provider through the real `runTurn`, not HTTP: replay after 10 newer turns, key conflict, 6 simultaneous identical sends → one answer and exactly 2 outbound calls, Clear during inference → 409 and nothing written, 7 hostile/malformed outputs, provider on/off equivalence, hang → both calls aborted and recorded indeterminate), `e2e:coach` 62 over HTTP.
- **Live provider** (`coach:interpreter:probe`, 6 synthetic messages, no student data): `.1` 2/6 valid (shape drift, rejected as designed); `.2` 6/6 valid, 0.8–1.2 s. One reading is arguable: "could you possibly sort out my afternoon somehow" → `availability` (a handoff only; nothing is written). Not proof of general language understanding.
- **Browser** (Playwright MCP, ordinary student, 1280 px and 390 px): typo request with no selection → two specific choices → choose → question prefilled → "Move it" asks the day → "Sunday" → exact preview with time → second tab opened → correction "No, not Sunday, Saturday" → old preview refused in the second tab with no write → lost confirm response (request aborted after commit) → page says pressing Confirm again is safe → one committed move, revision 2 → second Confirm replays the receipt (1 `moved` event, 0 `deferred`) → "Open it in Calendar" selects the task on its new week → Home loads → reload keeps the receipt. 390 px overflow fixed. IME composition Enter does not send. Teaching-only declined, mixed request answered + declined, study-method help answered. Console clean except the deliberately aborted request.
- **Not proven in the browser**: scored fixture result/repair and practice result selection (covered earlier over HTTP in `coach:selection:test`, not re-driven here), a model outage in the page (covered by the simulated runtime suite), lost *send* response (proven in slice 1 and by the runtime replay test, not repeated here).

Performance (dev server, `TRACE_READS=1`, cold first sample inflates max; n = 5–6 per class; `scripts/coach-timing-measure.ts`):
| class | p50 before → after | reads |
|---|---|---|
| preview ("move it to tomorrow") | 5.7 s → 1.0 s | 372 → 20 |
| interpreted (live model) | 8.7 s → 1.9 s | 367 → 15 |
| confirmation ("yes") | 0.6 s | 7 |
| simple "what next?" (heavy path) | **4.4 s, unchanged** | 365 |
Attribution (reads per source, tagged per call): shared operating state 220, evidence pack 57, revision queue 56, tracker 16, studyWeek 4. The Coach now skips them for intents that do not read them (`today`, `move_block`, `availability`, `log_*`, `exam`). The heavy path still needs the shared operating state; making that service cheaper is a separate piece of work.

Migration state: `20261218` applied to staging; `beta:baseline` regenerated (105 migrations); install rehearsal passes (function grants 203/203); restore rehearsal 10/10; dry-run shows 0 pending. `confirm_coach_proposal` and `set_task_execution` were not modified.

Remaining / limits (stated, not hidden):
- Calendar's own `moveTask` (TypeScript) is still a read-then-write; the Coach's move is atomic in SQL with the same rules. The two can drift: a test that runs both against the same fixtures is not written. A protected commitment or class recorded in `calendar_commitments` is avoided at preview but not re-checked at confirm (mocks/bookings are, by the existing guard); a race there is possible.
- Day-only and "no free slot" fall back to a labelled day placement; soft task overlaps are never created by the Coach, so Calendar's acknowledgement path is not exercised.
- Tentative-vs-booked exam, availability and class-change previews are still handoffs. Rehearsal/appointment and LOS selection are not added (reading is).
- Provider cancellation is proven with a simulated hang and by the OpenAI SDK's `signal` for the live provider, not by a live hang. A process crash after the provider accepted a request can still bill without an answer; it is recorded as indeterminate on takeover, not prevented.
- Two-device races: confirm/clear and confirm/dismiss are proven at the database / conditional-write level; no two-browser run of Clear-versus-Confirm.
- Hinglish: two phrases and "kal" (asks). No general multilingual claim.
- The heavy-path latency above; `/progress` heartbeats and the 23.5 s run-state outlier from §9.29 remain unclassified.


### 9.31 Lifecycle closeout and Coach safety (13 Nov 2026)
**Done (disposable dev project, uncommitted):**
- One move boundary: migrations `20261219_task_move_boundary` + `20261220_task_move_event_type`. `apply_task_move` takes the per-student scope lock first, then validates owner, revision, cancelled/recorded/continued, cycle, past day, protected commitments/classes (incl. overnight and moved occurrences) and mocks (BK409), writes the task and its event. Calendar `moveTask` and Coach `confirm_coach_move` both call it; the Coach adds its own restrictions (not started, not a paper/re-check, saved study window, strict no-overlap). Calendar keeps task-to-task overlap as a warned soft overlap. `npm run task:move:db:test -- --confirm-target=asylsxszdhlnzewhwtga`: 29 checks (parity matrix, class-vs-confirm race in both winning orders, two simultaneous moves into one slot, rollback on event failure).
- Found from the live schema: `task_events_event_type_check` never allowed `'placed'`, so Calendar's reopen-a-deferred-task move wrote no history event (error logged and swallowed). Now recorded as `moved` with `reopened:true`.
- Slot picker uses the student's saved weekday windows (06:00–07:00 student is never offered 08:00; no windows or no gap → day-only, stated as such). Exact dates and times resolve; an exact time is never replaced, a taken one gets the nearest feasible alternative.
- Semantic boundary and complex-request review (see COACH-PROMPTS.md); `repair` selection binds chat to its own source paper; "What next?"/"today" lead with Home's `state.nextAction` (`today` left the lean path, so it is slower).
- `createStudent` registers the account before sign-in/onboarding and deletes it on failure; `preflightServer`.
- Lifecycle runner `scripts/e2e-lifecycle.ts` (`npm run lifecycle`, `lifecycle:a|b|c`), evidence JSON in `docs/evidence/`.
**Results:** A (fixture bank) 42/42; A (actual bank) 11 pass + 1 UNAVAILABLE; B 12 pass, 1 not run; C 11 pass, 1 not run. Endpoint-level only.

They prove the app's rules answer correctly from consistent older history; they do not prove a real week elapsed, recurrence, reminders or cron. The planner sizes a first plan by what is left of the day, so the same profile gives next week's cycle (afternoon), one reading, or four tasks depending on the clock; the runner adapts and records which happened. Only one fixture bank at a time (lock); the actual-bank track now reports synthetic fixture items from another worker as contamination, never as capacity.

**Results (this run):**

### 9.35 Settings closeout 2: app-wide effects and account safety (14 Nov 2026)
Continues §9.33 on the disposable dev project. Two ADDITIVE migrations applied through the guarded runner (only mine were pending; Journey B added none): `20261221_setup_commit_checked` (wraps the existing `onboarding_commit_step`, never copies its body) and `20261222_classes_finished` (two nullable/defaulted columns on `calendar_commitments` + `set_classes_finished`). Nothing committed. Clean-install/upgrade/restore rehearsal of these (and of 20261219/20) was NOT performed (no isolated-database rehearsal exists for them).

**Fixed / built**
- **Session authentication.** `recentAuth` now counts only authenticating `amr` methods (password, otp, magiclink, oauth, totp, sso/saml, mfa, recovery, invite); `token_refresh`, `anonymous`, unknown and absent methods never count (old password + recent refresh is NOT recent). 11 pure checks.
- **ICS escaping.** `escapeText` emitted a bare `;` (a `'\;'` literal bug that the expected string repeated). Fixed; asserted as exact bytes (`5C 3B`) and on the wire.
- **Calendar export.** Booked mock appointments are exported from their own schedule (task-free, future, classes opt-in does not hide them; a mock task on the same date is not a second event); `loadCalendarWeek({strict})` makes every material read failure throw so the export fails closed; recurring class occurrences keep their ORIGINAL slot identity across a move and two moved onto one date stay two events (`exportKey`, additive); exact stored instants preserve an overnight one-off end and both instants of the repeated DST hour. 26 HTTP checks incl. independent parser.
- **Setup versions.** Completed-user edits send the version the editor read; compare-and-increment happens in the same transaction under the profile lock. Two overlapping saves: exactly one commits, the other gets 409 `setup_changed` + current version, input kept; a stale retry of a committed save is refused (never double-applied); cosmetic name edits do not move the version; first-time setup is unaffected. The saved version is returned and the plan impact belongs to it; an impact-read failure after commit is reported as "saved, impact unavailable". 13 HTTP checks + browser two-tab test.
- **Classes finished / undo.** One transaction ends every active weekly class series on the effective date (previous end remembered), sets the profile date, leaves history, logs, the accepted cycle and tasks untouched; prospective cycles stop being class-anchored (schedule, next-week and review readers honour `ends_on`); undo restores exactly the series it ended, never an archived one; idempotent. The calendar export follows both. 12 HTTP checks + browser.
- **Deletion.** Owned Storage objects (under `<user id>/`) are removed through the Storage API first (none exist today: no uploads feature, no student write policy; a student upload is refused by RLS); a failure leaves the account intact; audit-write failure after deletion can no longer change a success into "nothing removed"; reauth returns to `/account`; lost-response copy warns not to "check" with Google (can create a new account).
- **Reminders.** Preference write committed + re-read failed → `saved, confirmed:false` (UI reloads); `syncReminders` fails closed on any unreadable source (no cancel/create/revive); send-time eligibility re-evaluates the actual review gate for that cycle; per-job state changes are guarded by the claim lease (a late worker cannot overwrite a newer claim); an unreadable plan state holds the job instead of deciding; a cancelled job revives when its mock moves back. Real worker rules proven on one student's own claimed jobs with a fake transport (no mail): 21 checks.
- `/api/profile`: unchanged beyond §9.33 (name-only is the canonical UI use; the multi-field legacy branch remains for tests and is validate-first with an honest `partial` reply, not yet a single atomic command).

**Real-session proof** (`account-session-aging-test`, 12 minutes of real time, 17/0): session A signed in at the start; after the window device B signed in; A's deletion was REFUSED with nothing deleted; per-device sign-out left another device signed in; A re-authenticated; a real database failure during the cascade (a test trigger, dropped afterwards) returned DELETE_FAILED with account, preferences, jobs and history intact; the retry deleted; the deleted student's Storage object was removed and another student's kept; afterwards both browser sessions were denied, a still-UNEXPIRED token read nothing and could not write, refresh failed, a repeat request answered "no longer signed in", the audit held exactly one failed and one deleted pseudonymous row. Limit: access tokens last an hour, so a `token_refresh` was not naturally produced inside the 12 minutes (that case is covered by the pure probe).

**Browser (Playwright, desktop and 390px, 6/6 each, 0 unexpected console/page errors):** original Settings journey plus two-tab stale setup edit (stale tab refused, typed value kept, nothing written, reload then commit) and class-led finished/undo with both downloads parsed.

**App-wide effect matrix (what is proven):** name → no version change; exam tentative→booked → committed with version, the exam read model reports the date, Home/Calendar/Curriculum/Tests/Coach render, Coach links the exam page, no attempt/form touched; availability/sitting limit → profile and windows change, new previews read them; classes finished/undo → series, cycle anchor and export (above). NOT proven: field-by-field Home/Curriculum/Coach content comparison (render + read model only), reported coverage/uncertainty edits' effect on planning, stale-proposal invalidation by a setup edit beyond the existing version mechanism, an accepted-task conflict review action after an availability change.

**Not done / limits:** browser-driven forced deletion failure, stale-session deletion and cross-device removal (proven at route level with real sessions instead); keyboard/IME/focus audit beyond Escape/Cancel/focus-return; reminder "saved but follow-up unavailable" and "source unreadable" cannot be injected without a public flag, so those paths are covered by code and the pure `reviewIsReady` check only; no provider-readiness vs scheduler-readiness status in the UI (the page only says delivery is not switched on); Google OAuth/reset-email return paths and any non-password reauth were not exercised (no provider provisioned); personal export unchanged (non-snapshot limit stated); no global rewrite of ownership checks (the narrow boundary is `auth.getUser()` + owner-scoped rows). Retention/legal: only technical statements were corrected (pseudonymous hash, backups age out on the provider's own schedule, a delivered email cannot be recalled); no retention durations or legal claims were invented, and the retention/legal text remains a draft awaiting the owner's decision.

### 9.36 B2: partial-scope safety, bank counting, migration rehearsal (14 Nov 2026) — PARTIAL closeout
Nothing committed. **Journey B is NOT marked fully complete:** its onboarding and assessment-to-review chain are still API-driven; the browser B spec remains partial (§9.34). This pass fixed the correctness gap and the small controls; the large UI items below were not built.

**Done**
- **A bounded partial is not testable scope.** A class item logged "part of it" no longer enters `targets` (it used to, at weight 0.5, so a whole outcome could be asked about after one part was taught). `buildCompletedScope` now lists it in `pendingPortions` (outcome, reading, session, date) until the whole outcome is confirmed by a later class (all of it / continued) or a study task for the outcome that is not the class's own consolidation. Completing the class's consolidation of the part does NOT unlock it (`classBlockSessions` from `plan_blocks.class_session_id`). Pending portions feed the scope revision hash, so preflight, reservation and a reserved paper see the change. Visible in the Tests scope panel ("Part of a class, not yet testable") and in the class-log form when "Part of it" is chosen. A free-text note is never used to choose questions. No migration. Weights for full/continued are unchanged.
- **Unsupported (stated, not claimed):** concept-level confirmation inside an outcome (one studied concept, one not). The model has no per-class concept record; building it needs a column/table, an `apply_class_log` patch, API/UI and assembly filtering. Until then the safe policy is the whole outcome stays pending. The "two concepts, one unstudied" test therefore holds only in the conservative form (neither concept of a part-only outcome is asked); a concept-level exclusion test does not exist.
- **Fixture mislabel fixed:** the "basic EPS only" note was on `r028-b` (expense recognition). The catalogue puts basic/diluted EPS under `r028-d`; runner and browser spec now use `r028-d` as the partial and a, b, c as full.
- **Tests:** `tests:scope:test` 65/0 (pending portion, consolidation-does-not-unlock, later class confirms, study task confirms, revision hash; a 160-outcome scope stops at 90 with the remainder named and nothing outside scope asked). Journey B runner on the fixture bank: **31 passed, 0 failed, `complete`** (scope = three taught outcomes + visible pending EPS portion; items only from those three). One assertion changed: Keep-for-later now requires at least one thread and unchanged threads, because three outcomes yield one thread, not two.
- **`scripts/lib/bankMode.ts`:** eligible/held-out inventory is read with pagination (a 1000-row page cap no longer truncates), failed pages are setup failures, held-out is counted against current-version approval; a fixture record now fails unless every item has an approval for its CURRENT version (status `reviewed` alone no longer passes).
- **Migration rehearsal:** `npm run beta:upgrade:rehearse` (extends the existing install rehearsal with `--upgrade`): baseline + checksum runner applies 20261219–20261222 on a throwaway local Postgres; ledger 109 rows, 0 mismatches, and tables/columns/constraints/functions/policies/triggers/views/RLS/grants all equal the live schema. Fresh-install of the baseline plus upgrade is proven; a restore-from-backup step for these four is not separately run.
- **A evidence negative control restored** by rerunning `--scenario=A --bank=fixture --inject=evidence`: expected failure, exit 1 (36 passed, 1 failed on the evidence-exactness check), evidence file `lifecycle-2026-11-14-A-fixture-inject-evidence-lcmwgpxzekogh.json`.

**Not done (open)**
- Calendar occurrence-versus-series editing (no UI; backend `scope:'occurrence'` exists only as a bare exception write), impact preview, accepted-work recovery, replay and restore-conflict handling; moved-occurrence versus recurring-pattern cycle tests.
- Browser-driven B: class-pattern onboarding through the UI, the paper/result/Keep-for-later/repair/review UI on a class-led student, revisionary and mixed preflight UI, exact-date/time Coach movement and drag, focus/IME checks, tightened console allowances, A mobile rerun on the final helper state.
- Uncertain-selection draft recovery in the class-log form (no durable draft state added).
- Explicit student-chosen reading baseline check; the cross-page unstable-concept rollover test; second after-spacing re-check inside B; controlled failing Coach provider.
- Actual-bank capacity unchanged: B on the real bank remains the limited control (not a scored journey); all scored B proof is fixture-bank software proof.
